Hey,

Got a new map here, it's good to be used for ZM/HG/DM. Do be beware that this is a big map and if you want it smaller you can (or I) block the second island off. All the rocks are glitch free, you cant swim under the island and there are invisible barriers around the map. It's located just behind the red bridge (where I make most of my maps :P)
All the vehicles are outside the boundaries, thus not reachable.

I also got a version of this with object locations for hunger games or w/e

Credits go to Moody as someone registered with my IG name Moody and never did anything with it.

Pics:












Code:
